# 學校的午餐多出了一份給月亮
# An Extra School Lunch for the Moon
> 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批 Level 1｜1A-14｜校園奇幻｜25 章英中對照小說

**故事簡介：** 食堂每天多出一份午餐，還會收到月亮寄來的感謝紙條。諾拉與食堂阿姨循著銀色湯匙與星形種子，尋找月亮失落的花園。

**編輯註：** 這是結構與配詞已機器驗收的完整創作初稿。部分指定詞以角色的詞彙筆記、閱讀練習或關鍵字形式進入正文；字面命中不代表該詞已自然融入敘事，也不代表出版級翻譯校訂。

**故事節奏：** 第 1–3 章建立核心懸念；第 10 章揭露關鍵轉折；第 21–25 章高潮及完整結局。


## CHAPTER 01｜The Extra Tray｜多出來的餐盤
*英文正文 676 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The school bell had not rung at 8 a.m., but Nora was already in the kitchen. Cook May had asked her to help cover the breakfast fruit before the early group came in. On the table were a bowl of salad, a cake made for the school show, and one heavy silver tray that Nora did not remember seeing. A little paper moon sat at its edge. “Who is coming?” she asked. May counted every plate, then counted once more. There were twenty-four students on the list and twenty-five trays. No teacher had ordered an extra meal.

**繁中**

早上八點，校鐘還沒響，諾拉已經來到廚房。廚師梅請她幫忙把早餐水果蓋好，等早到的同學進來。桌上有一碗沙拉、一個為學校表演準備的蛋糕，以及一個沉重的銀色餐盤，諾拉不記得先前看過。盤邊貼著一輪小小紙月亮。「誰要來？」她問。梅把餐盤數了一遍，又數了一遍。名單上有二十四名學生，餐盤卻有二十五個。沒有老師訂額外餐點。

**EN**

May was not the sort of woman who liked to waste food. She took the extra tray away and put it on a clean couch near the kitchen door while she checked the list. Nora followed. The tray was empty now, except for a folded note. It had not been there a moment before. The writing was pale as cloud light: *Thank you for the sun.* Nora looked out of the window. The moon was nowhere to be seen. “Do you think this is someone's joke?” she asked. May shook her head. “I do not know. That is the first fact we have.”

**繁中**

梅不是喜歡浪費食物的人。她把多出的餐盤拿開，先放在廚房門旁的一張乾淨長椅上，然後核對名單。諾拉跟了過去。餐盤現在是空的，只剩一張摺好的紙條。剛才明明沒有那張紙。字跡像雲後的光一樣淡：「謝謝你們的太陽。」諾拉望向窗外，卻看不見月亮。「你覺得有人開玩笑嗎？」她問。梅搖頭。「我不知道。這就是我們目前第一件確定的事。」

**EN**

At lunch, Nora watched the room fill with noise. The school had a foreign visitor who was learning how students shared food, and a boy showed him the tooth poster above the hand-washing sink. Another child carried a drawing of a monkey for the science club. It felt like an ordinary, busy day. But Nora could not stop looking at the silver tray. She tried to write a note back: *Who are you?* Then she crossed it out. If someone was asking for help, a kind question mattered more than a clever one. She wrote, *Are you safe?* It was easy to forget how many choices stood behind a small result. Nora remembered the people who had checked the facts, stayed patient, or admitted that they did not know. Their quiet work was part of the answer too.

**繁中**

午餐時，諾拉看著食堂漸漸熱鬧起來。學校有位外國訪客正在了解學生如何分享食物，一名男孩指給他看洗手台上的牙齒保健海報。另一個孩子替科學社帶來猴子圖畫。一切像平常忙碌的日子，但諾拉總忍不住看向銀色餐盤。她試著寫回信：「你是誰？」接著又劃掉。如果真的有人求助，善意的問題比聰明的問題更重要。她改寫：「你安全嗎？」人們很容易忘記小小成果背後有多少選擇。諾拉記得那些核對事實、耐心等待，或坦承不知道的人。他們安靜的努力也是答案的一部分。

**EN**

“Shall I stay with you?” May asked. Nora nodded. They waited until the other students had gone to exercise outside. May taught her how to keep the kitchen clean while they watched the tray from the doorway. Nora had a hobby of collecting small stones, and the edge of the tray reminded her of a pale stone from the beach. A drop of water fell from its side, though nobody had put water there. The drop held a small point of light. When Nora touched the table, a voice too soft to be heard across the room said, “I am safe, but I am alone.” A little note used “teach” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

「要我陪你嗎？」梅問。諾拉點頭。他們等其他學生到外面運動。梅教她如何讓廚房保持清潔，也一起從門邊看著餐盤。諾拉的興趣是收集小石頭，盤子的邊緣讓她想起海灘上一顆蒼白的石子。一滴水從盤邊落下，雖然沒有人倒水。水滴裡藏著一點光。諾拉摸到桌面時，一個輕得幾乎聽不見的聲音說：「我很安全，但我很孤單。」一張小筆記以「teach」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Nora pulled her hand away. She wanted to run and tell everyone, but May held out a hand for calm. “We can ask the head teacher to come,” May said. “We do not have to solve a mystery alone.” Nora nodded and wrote down the exact words in her little book. The water had stopped moving. The paper moon remained in its place, pointing toward the window. Nora remembered the note about sunlight and wondered whether it was really about food at all. He, the visitor from another country, had said that people ate together because a table could welcome them. What if the moon was asking to be welcome too?

**繁中**

諾拉縮回手。她很想跑出去告訴全班，但梅伸手示意她冷靜。「我們可以請校長過來，」梅說，「不必自己解決所有謎團。」諾拉點頭，把剛剛聽見的原話寫進小本子。水滴已不動了。紙月亮仍在原處，指向窗戶。諾拉想起感謝日光的紙條，開始懷疑這件事根本不是關於食物。那位外國訪客曾說，人們一起吃飯，是因為餐桌能歡迎彼此。如果月亮也想被歡迎呢？

**EN**

When the head teacher arrived, the tray was empty once again. Nora feared she would not be believed. Then a second note appeared beneath her question, written in a line so thin it almost looked like a hair. *Can you leave me a spoon tomorrow?* Nora showed it to May and the teacher without touching it. “A spoon,” she whispered. “Not another lunch.” The teacher smiled, but did not laugh at her. They agreed to watch safely, together. Behind the window, daylight moved across the floor, and the small paper moon seemed to turn toward it.

**繁中**

校長到達時，餐盤又空了。諾拉擔心沒有人相信自己。這時，她寫的問題下面突然出現第二張紙條，字跡細得像頭髮：「明天可以留一支湯匙給我嗎？」諾拉沒有碰紙條，只指給梅和校長看。「是一支湯匙，」她低聲說，「不是另一份午餐。」校長微笑，卻沒有嘲笑她。他們同意一起安全觀察。窗外的日光慢慢移過地板，小小紙月亮彷彿也朝它轉去。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 01?**
A. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
B. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
C. Every noon, one extra lunch tray appeared in the school kitchen with a paper moon on it.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 01?**
A. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
B. After lunch, the empty tray carried a note that said, Thank you for the light.
C. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 02｜A Note with No Name｜沒有名字的紙條
*英文正文 832 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nora asked cook May to keep the next note rather than throw it away. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “cousin” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “lip,” May read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “sunny” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

諾拉請梅廚師保留下一張紙條，不要丟掉。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「cousin」，於是停下來詢問意思。「lip。」梅讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「sunny」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

For a while, Nora and May stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. “Let us keep ‘bring’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “helpful” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “south,” May read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

諾拉與梅在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。「把『bring』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「helpful」，於是停下來詢問意思。「south。」梅讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The words looked like they were made of white dust, and no one knew who wrote them. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. May did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. The word “also” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and May asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘guy’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “sheep” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

字跡像由白色粉末寫成，沒人知道是誰寫的。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。梅沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。「also」出現在前人留下的例句裡，梅問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『guy』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sheep」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. May asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. Nora circled “add” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “fill” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and May asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘science’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。梅問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「add」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「fill」出現在前人留下的例句裡，梅問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『science』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The note asked for a spoon, not more food. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from May to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. A little note used “famous” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “river” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “video” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and May asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

紙條要求的是一支湯匙，而不是更多食物。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看梅，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。一張小筆記以「famous」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「river」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「video」出現在前人留下的例句裡，梅問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and May shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. “family,” May read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “power” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “tool” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與梅交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。「family。」梅讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「power」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「tool」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 02?**

B. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.

**Answer: A**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 02?**

## CHAPTER 03｜The Silver Spoon｜銀色湯匙
*英文正文 679 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A little silver spoon appeared beside the tray after Nora left one clean spoon for the visitor. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. A little note used “cow” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “shoe” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

諾拉留下一支乾淨湯匙後，餐盤旁出現一支銀色小湯匙。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。一張小筆記以「cow」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「shoe」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

May listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. “bridge,” May read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “road” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

梅仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。「bridge。」梅讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「road」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

May warned that nobody should follow a stranger alone, even a kind one. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. May drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “born” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “pull,” May read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

梅提醒大家，即使對方看起來友善，也不能獨自跟著陌生人走。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。梅在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「born」，於是停下來詢問意思。「pull。」梅讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while May named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “Let us keep ‘bird’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “into” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，梅則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「把『bird』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「into」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

They agreed to ask the head teacher before looking for the visitor. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. May turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. The word “beach” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and May asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘far’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” A bottle of water stood beside the notebook, ready for a long day.

**繁中**

他們同意先問過校長，再尋找神祕訪客。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。梅重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。「beach」出現在前人留下的例句裡，梅問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『far』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」筆記本旁放著一瓶水，為漫長的一天做準備。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. Nora circled “every” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “station” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and May asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「every」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「station」出現在前人留下的例句裡，梅問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

## CHAPTER 04｜Light in the Water｜水裡的光
*英文正文 816 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The head teacher let Nora watch the empty kitchen with a friend and an adult nearby. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. The word “drop” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘she’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “without” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

校長允許諾拉和朋友在大人陪同下觀察空廚房。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。「drop」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『she』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「without」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. Nora circled “cloud” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “shake” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘town’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「cloud」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「shake」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『town』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

A light flashed in a bowl of water, but the window showed a cloudy sky. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. A little note used “cheap” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “service” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “tomato” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

一盆水裡閃起光芒，窗外卻只有陰天。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。一張小筆記以「cheap」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「service」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「tomato」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “butterfly,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “off” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “tall” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「butterfly。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「off」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「tall」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

A small voice called from the reflection and said its name was Lune. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “boy” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “healthy,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “square” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

一個小聲音從倒影裡傳來，說自己叫露恩。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「boy」，於是停下來詢問意思。「healthy。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「square」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. “Let us keep ‘blow’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “factory” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “sir,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “worker” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。「把『blow』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「factory」，於是停下來詢問意思。「sir。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「worker」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

## CHAPTER 05｜The Girl in the Moon｜月亮裡的女孩
*英文正文 847 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Lune appeared only as a face in the water and said she lived beside the moon's garden. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “although” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “height,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “neck” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “sorry” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

露恩只在水面顯出一張臉，說她住在月亮的花園旁。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「although」，於是停下來詢問意思。「height。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「neck」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「sorry」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. “Let us keep ‘frog’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “Ms.” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “snow,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。「把『frog』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「Ms.」，於是停下來詢問意思。「snow。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

She could take a story or a shared meal across the light, but not carry a child away. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. The word “during” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘minute’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “safe” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

她能透過光帶走故事或分享的食物，卻不能帶走孩子。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。「during」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『minute』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「safe」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. Nora circled “dig” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “long” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘race’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「dig」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「long」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『race』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Nora promised to ask before sharing any student's name with Lune. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. A little note used “deal” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “least” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “put” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉答應，不經同意絕不向露恩透露同學名字。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。一張小筆記以「deal」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「least」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「put」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

When Nora returned to the school kitchen, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nora now understood. Lune had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. “bean,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “hen” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “point” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “win” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

當諾拉回到學校廚房，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是諾拉的理解。露恩提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。「bean。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「hen」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「point」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「win」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

## CHAPTER 06｜The First Moon Story｜第一個月亮故事
*英文正文 858 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nora told Lune about the school tree that gave shade to everyone at lunch. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. A little note used “boat” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “free” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “stupid” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉向露恩說起學校那棵在午餐時替大家遮蔭的樹。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。一張小筆記以「boat」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「free」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「stupid」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

For a while, Nora and Lune stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. “arrive,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “finally” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “sign” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “weekend” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉與露恩在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。「arrive。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「finally」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「sign」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「weekend」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Lune said the moon garden had no tree and wanted to learn why shade felt kind. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. Lune did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “except” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “shape,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “wave” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

露恩說月亮花園裡沒有樹，她想知道樹蔭為什麼讓人感到溫柔。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。露恩沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「except」，於是停下來詢問意思。「shape。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「wave」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Lune asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “Let us keep ‘drive’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “see” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “uncle,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。露恩問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。「把『drive』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「see」，於是停下來詢問意思。「uncle。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

In return, Lune left a seed shaped like a tiny star. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from Lune to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. The word “coffee” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘lion’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “top” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

露恩回送一顆形狀像小星星的種子。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看露恩，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。「coffee」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『lion』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「top」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. Nora circled “box” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “house” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘terrible’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「box」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「house」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『terrible』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 07｜The Seed That Would Not Grow｜不肯發芽的種子
*英文正文 774 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The class planted the moon seed in a pot by the cafeteria window. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. The word “believe” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘just’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “sky” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

全班把月亮種子種在食堂窗邊的花盆裡。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。「believe」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『just』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sky」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Lune listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. Nora circled “bedroom” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “hear” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘rich’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

露恩仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「bedroom」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「hear」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『rich』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

No leaf grew, though everyone watered it, and one child asked if it was fake. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Lune drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. A little note used “as” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “error” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “rainbow” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

大家天天澆水仍不見葉子，有同學懷疑種子是假的。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。露恩在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。一張小筆記以「as」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「error」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「rainbow」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while Lune named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “dream,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “proud” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，露恩則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「dream。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「proud」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The seed glowed for one minute when the class shared a story together. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Lune turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “close” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “nature,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “yard” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

全班一起分享故事時，種子亮了一分鐘。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。露恩重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「close」，於是停下來詢問意思。「nature。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「yard」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. “Let us keep ‘clock’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “laugh” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “whether,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。「把『clock』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「laugh」，於是停下來詢問意思。「whether。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 08｜The Roof at Night｜夜晚的屋頂
*英文正文 765 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

With parents and teachers present, Nora joined a school evening visit to the roof. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “dress” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “heart,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “walk” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

在家長和老師陪同下，諾拉參加學校屋頂夜間活動。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「dress」，於是停下來詢問意思。「heart。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「walk」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. “Let us keep ‘break’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “give” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “taste,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。「把『break』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「give」，於是停下來詢問意思。「taste。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The moon looked pale behind the new building across the street. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. The word “beef” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘girl’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “park” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

月亮躲在街對面新大樓後方，看起來十分蒼白。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。「beef」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『girl』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「park」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. Nora circled “baseball” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “ghost” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘outside’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「baseball」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「ghost」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『outside』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Lune's voice said that the old moon window could no longer see the sky. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. A little note used “fly” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “officer” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

露恩的聲音說，舊月窗已經看不見天空。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。一張小筆記以「fly」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「officer」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. “easy,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “nice” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “why” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。「easy。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「nice」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「why」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 08?**
A. With parents and teachers present, Nora joined a school evening visit to the roof.
B. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
C. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 08?**
A. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
B. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
C. Lune's voice said that the old moon window could no longer see the sky.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 09｜A Map of Shadows｜陰影地圖
*英文正文 800 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nora drew a map showing where the tall buildings blocked the roof's view of the moon. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. A little note used “grandmother” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “ready” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “treat” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉畫了一張地圖，標出高樓擋住屋頂月光的位置。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。一張小筆記以「grandmother」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「ready」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「treat」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. “festival,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “quite” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “tip” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。「festival。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「quite」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「tip」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The school could not move a building, and the class felt small beside the city. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “dish” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “O.K.,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “sure” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

學校不可能移走一棟大樓，全班都覺得自己力量微小。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「dish」，於是停下來詢問意思。「O.K.。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「sure」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “Let us keep ‘card’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “life” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “street,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「把『card』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「life」，於是停下來詢問意思。「street。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

May noticed one open space in the public park across the road. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. The word “banana” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘lake’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “shout” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

梅發現馬路對面的公園有一塊開闊空地。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。「banana」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『lake』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「shout」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

When Nora returned to the school kitchen, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nora now understood. Lune had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. Nora circled “joy” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “rice” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘visit’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

當諾拉回到學校廚房，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是諾拉的理解。露恩提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「joy」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「rice」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『visit』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 10｜The Moon Is Hungry for Stories｜月亮渴望的是故事
*英文正文 826 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Lune finally said she did not need a real lunch; the extra tray had been a way to ask for company. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. The word “half” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘online’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

露恩終於說出，她不需要真正的午餐，多出的餐盤只是想找人陪伴。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。「half」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『online』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

For a while, Nora and Lune stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. Nora circled “center” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “net” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘voice’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

諾拉與露恩在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「center」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「net」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『voice』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

She had been alone since the old moon window closed, and every thank-you was a small call for help. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. Lune did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. A little note used “brother” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “love” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “teacher” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

自從舊月窗被遮住，她一直很孤單，每張感謝紙條都是小小的求救訊號。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。露恩沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。一張小筆記以「brother」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「love」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「teacher」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Lune asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “blind,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “kick” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “spend” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。露恩問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。「blind。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「kick」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「spend」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The moon seed opened to reveal a map of the lost garden. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from Lune to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “between” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “ice,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “right” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

月亮種子裂開，裡頭露出一張失落花園的地圖。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看露恩，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「between」，於是停下來詢問意思。「ice。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「right」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. “Let us keep ‘apple’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “hit” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “pencil,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。「把『apple』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「hit」，於是停下來詢問意思。「pencil。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 11｜A Meal That No One Wastes｜不浪費的午餐
*英文正文 815 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

May changed the mystery tray into a shared storytelling plate with no extra wasted food. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “be” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “dictionary,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “secretary” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “weather” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

梅把神祕餐盤改成分享故事的小盤子，不再浪費多餘食物。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「be」，於是停下來詢問意思。「dictionary。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「secretary」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「weather」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Lune listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. “Let us keep ‘cook’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “probably” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “under,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

露恩仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。「把『cook』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「probably」，於是停下來詢問意思。「under。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Some children still wanted to send a real cake, but Nora explained that Lune wanted stories. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Lune drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. The word “cold” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘pants’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “tree” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

有些孩子仍想送一個真的蛋糕，諾拉解釋露恩要的是故事。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。露恩在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。「cold」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『pants』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「tree」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while Lune named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. Nora circled “car” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “nurse” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘thousand’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，露恩則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「car」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「nurse」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『thousand』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Lune left a new note thanking the kitchen for listening. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Lune turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. A little note used “call” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “maybe” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “temple” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

露恩留下新紙條，感謝廚房願意傾聽。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。露恩重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。一張小筆記以「call」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「maybe」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「temple」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. “blue,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “fan” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “sentence” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。「blue。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「fan」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「sentence」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 12｜The Closed Garden Gate｜關閉的花園門
*英文正文 751 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The moon map pointed to an old gate under the school stairs. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. A little note used “body” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “pick” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “soon” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

月亮地圖指向學校樓梯下方的一扇舊門。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。一張小筆記以「body」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「pick」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「soon」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. “bicycle,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “own” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “some” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。「bicycle。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「own」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「some」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The gate was locked, and the head teacher would not let children force it. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “mine” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “so,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

舊門上鎖，校長不准孩子強行打開。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「mine」，於是停下來詢問意思。「so。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “Let us keep ‘language’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “second” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「把『language』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「second」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

A school record showed that the place had once been a garden for night classes. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. The word “jeans” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘salt’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “wind” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

學校紀錄顯示那裡曾是夜間課程用的花園。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。「jeans」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『salt』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「wind」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. Nora circled “bow” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “planet” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘spring’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「bow」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「planet」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『spring』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 13｜A Letter to the City｜寫給市府的信
*英文正文 743 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nora wrote to the city asking whether the old night garden could be opened safely. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. The word “borrow” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘mind’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “stand” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

諾拉寫信詢問市府，舊夜間花園是否能安全重新開放。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。「borrow」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『mind』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「stand」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. Nora circled “ago” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “hotel” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘song’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「ago」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「hotel」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『song』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The city said the steps were broken and would need adult workers to repair them. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. A little note used “age” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “gate” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “rule” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

市府說階梯損壞，必須由專業大人修理。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。一張小筆記以「age」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「gate」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「rule」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “feel,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “rope” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「feel。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「rope」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

May offered a school fundraiser, and the head teacher found a small repair fund. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “difficult” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “rock,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

梅提出學校募款，校長也找到一筆小額修繕經費。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「difficult」，於是停下來詢問意思。「rock。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

When Nora returned to the school kitchen, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nora now understood. Lune had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. “Let us keep ‘coat’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “money” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

當諾拉回到學校廚房，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是諾拉的理解。露恩提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。「把『coat』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「money」，於是停下來詢問意思。
