## CHAPTER 14｜A Cloud Over the Park｜公園上方的雲
*英文正文 829 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A storm covered the park, so the planned moon evening had to be delayed. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “business” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “happy,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “rest” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

暴風雨籠罩公園，原訂月夜活動不得不延期。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「business」，於是停下來詢問意思。「happy。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「rest」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

For a while, Nora and Lune stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. “Let us keep ‘aunt’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “grow” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “relative,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

諾拉與露恩在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。「把『aunt』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「grow」，於是停下來詢問意思。「relative。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Nora feared Lune would think they had forgotten her. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. Lune did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. The word “already” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘glove’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “parent” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

諾拉擔心露恩以為大家忘了她。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。露恩沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。「already」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『glove』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「parent」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Lune asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. Nora circled “agree” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “first” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘paint’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。露恩問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「agree」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「first」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『paint』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The next tray carried a gentle note saying that safety mattered more than speed. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from Lune to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. A little note used “ever” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “lemon” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “uniform” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

下一個餐盤上有溫柔紙條，說安全比速度更重要。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看露恩，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。一張小筆記以「ever」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「lemon」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「uniform」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. “death,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “learn” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “shoulder” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。「death。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「learn」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「shoulder」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 15｜The Lost Plant｜失去的植物
*英文正文 751 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Workers found a small dry plant hidden behind the old garden gate. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. A little note used “job” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “sister” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

工人在舊花園門後找到一株乾枯的小植物。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。一張小筆記以「job」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「sister」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Lune listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. “hat,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “restaurant” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “want” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

露恩仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。「hat。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「restaurant」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「want」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Nora wanted to take it at once, but May said living things needed careful hands. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Lune drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “expensive” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “please,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “visitor” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

諾拉想馬上把植物帶走，梅說照顧生命要小心。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。露恩在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「expensive」，於是停下來詢問意思。「please。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「visitor」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while Lune named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “Let us keep ‘eat’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “pipe” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “T-shirt,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，露恩則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「把『eat』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「pipe」，於是停下來詢問意思。「T-shirt。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

A local gardener taught the class how to move it without harming the roots. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Lune turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. The word “animal” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘meal’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “sore” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

附近的園藝師教大家如何不傷根地移植。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。露恩重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。「animal」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『meal』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sore」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. Nora circled “market” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “slim” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「market」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「slim」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

## CHAPTER 16｜The Story Circle｜說故事的圓圈
*英文正文 769 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Each student brought one memory to the repaired garden, from a funny mistake to a kind hello. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. The word “fast” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘subject’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

每個同學都帶一段回憶到修好的花園，有好笑的錯誤，也有溫暖的招呼。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。「fast」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『subject』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. Nora circled “earth” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “smart” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘writer’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「earth」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「smart」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『writer』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

A quiet student worried that his story was too small to matter. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. A little note used “correct” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “know” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “wear” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

一位安靜的學生擔心自己的故事太小，不值得說。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。一張小筆記以「correct」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「know」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「wear」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “cookie,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “knee” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “up” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「cookie。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「knee」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「up」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Nora made room for him, and the moon seed shone brighter than before. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “cent” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “heat,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “television” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

諾拉讓出發言空間，月亮種子比以前更亮。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「cent」，於是停下來詢問意思。「heat。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「television」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. “Let us keep ‘angry’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “fire” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “tea,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。「把『angry』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「fire」，於是停下來詢問意思。「tea。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 17｜A Room for Everyone｜每個人的房間
*英文正文 804 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The city agreed that the garden could be used for supervised public evening events. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “air” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “married,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “reason” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

市府同意花園可供有大人管理的公共夜間活動使用。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「air」，於是停下來詢問意思。「married。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「reason」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. “Let us keep ‘finger’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “really” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “vegetable,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。「把『finger』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「really」，於是停下來詢問意思。「vegetable。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The school needed a clear schedule so no child felt forced to attend after dark. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. The word “face” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘possible’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “talk” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

學校需要明確時間表，確保沒有人被迫在晚上參加。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。「face」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『possible』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「talk」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. Nora circled “example” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “popcorn” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘strong’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「example」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「popcorn」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『strong』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Nora made a plan with both early and late visits so more families could join. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. A little note used “driver” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “order” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “smoke” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉安排較早與較晚兩個時段，讓更多家庭能參加。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。一張小筆記以「driver」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「order」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「smoke」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

When Nora returned to the school kitchen, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nora now understood. Lune had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. “decide,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “music” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “repeat” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

當諾拉回到學校廚房，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是諾拉的理解。露恩提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。「decide。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「music」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「repeat」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 17?**
A. The city agreed that the garden could be used for supervised public evening events.
B. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
C. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 17?**
A. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
B. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
C. Nora made a plan with both early and late visits so more families could join.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 18｜A Light in the Hall｜走廊裡的光
*英文正文 835 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The little star seed sent a thin beam of light down the school hall. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. A little note used “child” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “leader” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “team” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

小小星形種子往學校走廊射出一道細光。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。一張小筆記以「child」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「leader」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「team」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

For a while, Nora and Lune stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. “certain,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “jump” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “set” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

諾拉與露恩在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。「certain。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「jump」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「set」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

It led to a wall of old student drawings from years before Nora was born. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. Lune did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “grass” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “open,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “yesterday” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

光帶大家看到一面舊學生畫牆，那些畫作比諾拉出生還早。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。露恩沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「grass」，於是停下來詢問意思。「open。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「yesterday」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Lune asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “Let us keep ‘giant’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “nose” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “towel,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。露恩問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。「把『giant』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「nose」，於是停下來詢問意思。「towel。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

One drawing showed Lune holding the same silver spoon. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from Lune to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. The word “father” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘need’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “touch” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

其中一幅畫畫著露恩拿著同一支銀色湯匙。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看露恩，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。「father」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『need』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「touch」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. Nora circled “dance” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “Mr.” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘today’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「dance」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「Mr.」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『today』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 19｜The First Friends｜最初的朋友
*英文正文 755 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The head teacher found letters from the children who had once spoken to Lune through water. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. The word “bite” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘ham’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “shirt” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

校長找到以前曾透過水面與露恩說話的孩子留下的信。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。「bite」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『ham』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「shirt」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Lune listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. Nora circled “bee” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “ground” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘problem’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

露恩仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「bee」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「ground」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『problem』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Those students had grown up and moved away when the old garden closed. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Lune drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. A little note used “attack” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “eye” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “pretty” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

花園關閉後，那些孩子長大並搬走。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。露恩在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。一張小筆記以「attack」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「eye」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「pretty」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while Lune named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “dog,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “join” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，露恩則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「dog。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「join」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Nora asked the city if the former students could be invited to the new opening. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Lune turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “count” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “interview,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

諾拉詢問市府能否邀請那些舊學生來參加新開幕。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。露恩重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「count」，於是停下來詢問意思。「interview。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. “Let us keep ‘black’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “interested” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “year,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。「把『black』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「interested」，於是停下來詢問意思。「year。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 20｜The Empty Chair Returns｜空椅子回來了
*英文正文 786 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

An older visitor brought the little wooden chair she had used beside the moon window long ago. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “butter” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “hide,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “low” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

一位年長訪客帶來自己多年前坐在月窗旁的小木椅。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「butter」，於是停下來詢問意思。「hide。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「low」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. “Let us keep ‘below’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “hard” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “level,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。「把『below』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「hard」，於是停下來詢問意思。「level。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

She feared Lune would not remember her after so much time. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. The word “anything” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘front’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “husband” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

她害怕這麼久了，露恩已不記得自己。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。「anything」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『front』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「husband」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. Nora circled “abroad” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “egg” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘hundred’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「abroad」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「egg」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『hundred』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The silver spoon rang softly when the visitor whispered her childhood nickname. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. A little note used “doctor” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “hope” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “tail” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

訪客輕聲說出童年綽號時，銀湯匙發出柔和聲響。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。一張小筆記以「doctor」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「hope」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「tail」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. “cry,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “homework” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “machine” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。「cry。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「homework」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「machine」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 20?**
A. An older visitor brought the little wooden chair she had used beside the moon window long ago.
B. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
C. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 20?**
A. The group gives up without speaking to anyone.
B. Someone wins a prize that has nothing to do with the problem.
C. The silver spoon rang softly when the visitor whispered her childhood nickname.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 21｜The Light Test｜月光測試
*英文正文 758 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Workers tested the lights and railings before the public moon night. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. A little note used “busy” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “only” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

工人在公共月夜活動前測試照明與欄杆。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。一張小筆記以「busy」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「only」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. “bored,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “mouth” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “type” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。「bored。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「mouth」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「type」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

A broken lamp delayed the opening, but nobody asked the children to fix a dangerous wire. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “bathroom” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “get,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “tonight” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

壞掉的燈讓開幕延期，但沒人要求孩子去修危險電線。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「bathroom」，於是停下來詢問意思。「get。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「tonight」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “Let us keep ‘ball’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “from” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “smell,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「把『ball』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「from」，於是停下來詢問意思。「smell。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

When the lamp worked, the moon's light reached the dry plant for the first time. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. May said a good kitchen counted both meals and people. A plate mattered most when someone had a place at the table. The word “airplane” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘door’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “save” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

燈修好後，月光第一次照到那株乾枯的植物。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。梅說，好廚房不只計算餐點，也關心人。當一個人有位置坐下來時，餐盤才最有意義。「airplane」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『door』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「save」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

When Nora returned to the school kitchen, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nora now understood. Lune had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. Nora circled “cheese” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “piano” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

當諾拉回到學校廚房，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是諾拉的理解。露恩提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「cheese」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「piano」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

## CHAPTER 22｜A Note for Tomorrow｜留給明天的紙條
*英文正文 837 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Lune wrote that she could visit the garden only when the sky was open and people chose to share. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nora had been thinking about simple things in the school kitchen when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nora tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The moon notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. The word “dry” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘program’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “weak” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

露恩寫道，只有天空開闊、人們願意分享時，她才能造訪花園。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。諾拉原本在學校廚房想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。諾拉不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。月亮筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。「dry」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『program』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「weak」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

For a while, Nora and Lune stood beside the moon notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nora was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. Nora circled “down” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “plate” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘tiger’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

諾拉與露恩在月亮筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。諾拉漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「down」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「plate」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『tiger』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Nora worried the friendship would end on cloudy nights. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nora chose to admit what was still missing instead. Lune did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nora wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. A little note used “climb” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “near” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “sugar” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉擔心陰天時友情會中斷。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，諾拉卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。露恩沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比諾拉原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。一張小筆記以「climb」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「near」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「sugar」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The sound of the warm smell of fresh bread came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nora stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Lune asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the moon notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “bench,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “leg” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “spell” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣。有一分鐘，諾拉不再試著當什麼都懂的人。露恩問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進月亮筆記本，並約定誰負責。「bench。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「leg」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「spell」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

May reminded her that a promise can last even when friends cannot meet. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nora looked from Lune to the moon notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nora felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “bat” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “inch,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “somewhere” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

梅提醒她，朋友不能見面時，承諾仍可以持續。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。諾拉看看露恩，又看看月亮筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。諾拉同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「bat」，於是停下來詢問意思。「inch。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「somewhere」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The moon notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. “Let us keep ‘baby’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “grade” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “robot,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。月亮筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。「把『baby』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「grade」，於是停下來詢問意思。「robot。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 23｜The School Moon Night｜學校月夜
*英文正文 778 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Families arrived for the first public moon night with teachers and city staff watching the garden. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nora both. At the school kitchen, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The moon notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. Nora watched the silver light touch the edge of the water. It was a gentle kind of magic, the sort that waited for someone to listen. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “bottom” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “never,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “throat” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

家長、老師與市府工作人員一起來到花園，參加第一場公開月夜。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，諾拉兩者都遇上了。在學校廚房，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。月亮筆記本讓諾拉在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。諾拉看見銀光碰到水面邊緣。那是一種溫柔的魔法，會等待有人願意傾聽。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「bottom」，於是停下來詢問意思。「never。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「throat」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Lune listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nora to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. “Let us keep ‘kiss’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “shower” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

露恩仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。諾拉注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。「把『kiss』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「shower」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Clouds moved slowly across the moon, and everyone waited without asking for a miracle. Nora felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the extra lunch tray. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Lune drew a line across the moon notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. The word “hello” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘pot’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “yes” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

雲慢慢飄過月亮，大家靜靜等待，沒有強求奇蹟。諾拉一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是多出的餐盤的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。露恩在月亮筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。「hello」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『pot』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「yes」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

A fresh page in the moon notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nora wrote down what could happen next, while Lune named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. Nora circled “fine” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “polite” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘wife’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

月亮筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。諾拉寫下接下來可能發生的事，露恩則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「fine」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「polite」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『wife』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Then the water bowl lit up, and Lune greeted the old visitor by name. Once the surprise passed, Nora wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Lune turned back to the moon notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. A little note used “crazy” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “pie” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “which” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

接著水盆亮了，露恩叫出那位年長訪客的名字。驚喜過後，諾拉開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。露恩重新翻開月亮筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。一張小筆記以「crazy」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「pie」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「which」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nora remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the moon notebook, Nora wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. “come,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “office” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “understand” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，諾拉記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起月亮筆記本前，諾拉寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。「come。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「office」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「understand」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 24｜The Garden Under Two Skies｜兩片天空下的花園
*英文正文 770 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Lune showed the children a picture of her moon garden, where new light was already growing. Before anyone called the day strange, Nora had noticed how quiet the school kitchen could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the warm smell of fresh bread made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nora opened the moon notebook and began with what had really happened. A little note used “cellphone” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “invite” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “present” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

露恩向孩子們展示月亮花園的樣子，那裡已長出新的光。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，諾拉就注意到，當大家擔心時，學校廚房會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，新鮮麵包的溫暖香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。諾拉沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開月亮筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。一張小筆記以「cellphone」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「invite」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「present」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nora found this uncomfortable, especially with the extra lunch tray still waiting for an answer. Lune suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. “across,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “horse” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “number” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。諾拉覺得不安，尤其多出的餐盤還等著答案。露恩建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。「across。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「horse」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「number」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Nora understood that the two gardens could be friends without becoming the same place. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nora wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Lune asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “game” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “much,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

諾拉明白，兩座花園不必變成同一個地方也能當朋友。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。諾拉想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。露恩問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「game」，於是停下來詢問意思。「much。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The group returned to the moon notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nora asked Lune to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “Let us keep ‘finish’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “meet” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “store,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

大家重新看著月亮筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。諾拉請露恩用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「把『finish』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「meet」，於是停下來詢問意思。「store。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The dry plant opened a tiny white flower under the moon. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nora did not look away from that part. Lune asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. The word “date” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘kid’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “rainy” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

那株乾枯植物在月光下開出小小白花。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。諾拉沒有迴避這一部分。露恩問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。「date」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『kid』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「rainy」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nora felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nora closed the moon notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. Nora circled “copy” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “item” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘radio’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。諾拉雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。諾拉輕輕闔上月亮筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「copy」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「item」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『radio』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 24?**
A. The school closes forever without a discussion.
B. Lune showed the children a picture of her moon garden, where new light was already growing.
C. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 24?**
A. The dry plant opened a tiny white flower under the moon.
B. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
C. The school closes forever without a discussion.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 25｜Lunch for the Moon｜給月亮的午餐
*英文正文 816 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 21 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

At school the next day, one tray still carried a paper moon, but it held a story instead of extra food. The school kitchen had its own familiar rhythm. Nora knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nora looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the moon notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. The word “catch” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘man’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “sale” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

隔天學校裡仍有一個貼紙月亮的餐盤，但裡頭放的是故事，不是多餘食物。學校廚房有自己熟悉的節奏。諾拉熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。諾拉卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。月亮筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。「catch」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『man』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sale」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

The first reaction in the school kitchen was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nora felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Lune pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. Nora circled “boss” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “lucky” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘root’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

學校廚房裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。諾拉兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。露恩指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「boss」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「lucky」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『root』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Nora invited classmates to add their own notes without writing anyone else's private secret. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the school kitchen. Nora took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Lune time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. The moon looked far away, but its notes arrived with the quiet care of a neighbor. Nora kept wondering who had felt alone enough to write them. A little note used “airport” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nora circled “king” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “push” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Lune asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

諾拉邀請同學寫下自己的故事，不寫別人的祕密。麻煩是真實的，即使學校廚房裡並非每個人都看清了風險。諾拉退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓露恩有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。月亮看起來很遠，紙條卻帶著鄰居般的溫柔。諾拉一直在想，是誰孤單到需要寫下那些字。一張小筆記以「airport」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。諾拉在閱讀單上圈出「king」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「push」出現在前人留下的例句裡，露恩問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

May joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nora copied the answers into the moon notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “fun,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “prepare” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

梅聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？諾拉把答案抄進月亮筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「fun。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「prepare」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

At noon, a silver line appeared across the tray: Thank you for being my friends. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nora could follow. Lune warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nora agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “dirty” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “party,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “theater” as its key word; Nora kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

中午餐盤上出現銀色字跡：「謝謝你們成為我的朋友。」新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成諾拉能循著走的一條線。露恩提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。諾拉同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「dirty」，於是停下來詢問意思。「party。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「theater」為關鍵字；諾拉留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nora and Lune shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. That afternoon Nora looked at the extra tray without feeling afraid of its mystery. The stories would continue, even on days when the moon was behind clouds. She had learned that sharing a place at the table could be a little like making a home for someone far away. “Let us keep ‘cut’ in our word notes,” Nora said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the moon notebook, Nora found the word “noisy” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “sight,” Lune read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。諾拉與露恩交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。那天下午，諾拉望著多出的餐盤，已不再害怕它的神祕。即使月亮藏在雲後，故事仍能持續。她學到，願意分享餐桌上的一個位置，就像替遠方的人打造一個小小的家。「把『cut』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」諾拉說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在月亮筆記本的角落，諾拉看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「noisy」，於是停下來詢問意思。「sight。」露恩讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

A. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
B. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
C. At school the next day, one tray still carried a paper moon, but it held a story instead of extra food.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。
